Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a symplectic algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 358. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 910/2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by symplectic generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 358, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 910. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the symplectic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 34. The moduli space has dimension at most 926 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to symplectic theory, we decompose the problem into 34 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 926. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
